[10/12/2025 6:11 pm] .looksmax
**Bromantane **

(Stimulant-Adaptogen Hybrid)

The apex tier right now. Originally developed by the Soviets to accelerate recovery post-cosmonaut deployment. Bromantane upregulates tyrosine hydroxylase and dopamine transport, meaning long-term dopaminergic enhancement with minimal crash. It’s stimulating without being jittery. You stay locked, emotionally level, and physically resilient.

MoA: Enhances dopamine synthesis enzymes + modulates mitochondrial function.

Half-life: ~11-13 hours

Notes: Non-habit forming, clean wakefulness. Can stack with almost anything. No notable crash.


[10/12/2025 6:12 pm] .looksmax
**Semax Amidate**
(Synthetic Peptide / Neurotrophic & Anti-Fatigue)

A modified version of Semax with better penetration and longer effect. Boosts BDNF, supports working memory under stress, and regulates dopamine tone without depleting it. The perfect off-cycle cognitive stabilizer.

MoA: Enhances BDNF, dopamine regulation, stress resilience

Half-life: Short (~2-3 hours), but impact lasts longer

Bonus: Zero peripheral stim. Clean cognitive boost with neurorepair angles.


[10/12/2025 6:12 pm] .looksmax
I really recommend this one


[10/12/2025 6:12 pm] .looksmax
its for general brain health


[10/12/2025 6:12 pm] .looksmax
boosts cognition


[10/12/2025 6:12 pm] .looksmax
has no side effects


[10/12/2025 6:13 pm] .looksmax
and this


[10/12/2025 6:14 pm] .looksmax
**Memantine**

(NMDA Antagonist / Neuroprotective / Tolerance Modulator)

One of the most misunderstood drugs. Memantine is not a stim—it’s a neuroprotective that blocks excitotoxicity. It limits glutamate overstimulation, which preserves dopamine receptors from tolerance-related downregulation.

MoA: Non-competitive NMDA antagonist

Half-life: ~60-80 hours (very long)

Use Case: Combine with Adderall or similar to flatten tolerance curve

Bonus: May assist in habit reversal and compulsive loop suppression.

[03/01/2026 10:26 pm] iamjustwhiteeye


{Forwarded Message}
My interpretation of all 15 pages

To preface, the Tao encompasses all dualities, but I believe a part of it leans towards the ego. Taoism, while being centered around The Tao, makes note of the ego quite often, and I feel compelled to speak on that.

The Tao, also called The Way, The Path, and The Source, is the source and fundamental principle of the universe. What comprises The Tao cannot be elucidated in any way. It is everything and nothing. It is foggy and it is clear, it is light and it is dark, it is sharp and it is dull. Although, it cannot be seen and it is amorphous, what could The Tao be, I wonder. The Tao should serve as a harbinger of both fortune and misfortune. Put too much water in a bucket and it overflows, put too little water in the bucket and the amount is insufficient, in either scenario, you walk with the bucket, either carefully with heedful footsteps, or without much care, allowing the bucket to swing lightly in the air. Take this scenario and apply it to the ego. It is of human nature to seek pleasure, especially for the sake of the ego. Should an ego be weak, one shall seek to overfill their bucket and spill the pleasures they gave themselves, causing a regression back to unhappiness. Should an ego be weak, one shall seek to underfill their bucket and spill the little pleasure they gave themselves. Too much and too little both lead to unhappiness, but what leads to the ultimate misery is the unpurified subconscious, but what does it mean to have an impure subconscious? And how does one purify it? An impure subconscious, by my definition is, a subconscious misaligned with the ego. A subconscious that is not aligned with the ego leads to unawareness of the self, unhappiness, and worst of all, unawareness of the shadow. A person who is not aware of their shadow and mindlessly repudiates is the unhappy one, however awareness of the shadow self is merely the beginning of the purification of the subconscious. To purify, abandon all feelings of shame and look into what you believe is the darkest part of yourself, consider the possibilities you once rejected, now look into the past, what did you hate most about your past self? Why do you hate them? Why did your past self exist? How do the past versions of you influence you today? Witness your darkness and every moment to revel in it, bask in that darkness, that evil, and then make your way to the light. By taking these steps, you align your subconscious with your ego and have successfully begun the purification process. I will admit I went on a bit of a tangent, but understand you are The Tao, you are also nothing and everything, you are and you are not.
Originally sent: 30/12/2025 2:09 am

Slowly i started just enjoying the exprience of life, not that I didn't, well, I didnt have much to enjoy before. [08/01/2026 2:29 am] iamjustwhiteeye
Thats mostly 13 to 14, my cognitive and wisdom rapidly advance randomly, and 15 was one of them. Probably because I was around people who can listen and understand the depth i spoke at. Even though most didn't, just having people try to understand or understanding was reassuring. I wanted to learn about those people. Thats why I went to the MK server. To learn about people like me, those who think differently or at least have the same interest. I didnt care for manipulation or anything like that. I just wanted to understand them. And I did. But than I made a name for myself. As I unfortunately always do. At that time I think I started the whiteeye teachings, near the ending, people asked, I answered. To hold people hands and show them what they can be and can see. But I also was emotionally weak, not really, I just was really hard on myself. Then I had a year of bliss, something clicked in my head, something that showed me this world, so beautiful no matter how crazy or evil it is. I started writing a lot more, sharing. My teacher at the time, English teacher, encouraged me and even said he'd like to see me in a writing competition or just the things where you submit your writings. Because the depth and forms I wrote impressed him. I was proud that someone like a English teacher could say my writing was good. Not everything was great, but to me my mind couldn't be more the opposite. Everything was perfect.


[08/01/2026 2:39 am] iamjustwhiteeye
Whiteroom came to be, the guilded and whiteroom 5.0 discord. Atp I was really done with the ayanokojis. But I enjoyed myself, it was something new, to help people in different ways and share my thoughts independently. I enjoyed it. Just being able to listen to those who seeked and wanted, and for me to be allowed to give or listen. I met many, I heard many stories. Its weird, some saw me as a genius above many, but I never saw myself that way and never will. I am no one, and nothing, as I am someone and everything I want to be. Genius isn't one of them. I just got lucky to gain such wisdom and to learn and exprience such terrible and good situations. I always was grateful and appreciated them. My mind started to break tho, the more I seen, the more I could see, you start understanding a lot and questioning to yourself why nothing. Why not do when you understand. Many say I knew of jung or wrote things of jung writings, but I never read or knew him at the time. But his concepts allowed me to understand a lot, ofc to me Its obvious and I came to it naturally. Yet many spend years learning. You start seeing the disconnect from yourself and regular people. Not that im special, just different in a more uncommon way. I enjoyed myself, talking deeply about such subjects, and learning new things. I became more free the longer I lived that year. Yet I caged myself inside my own heart and head. I loved myself, but for the reasons I did, they had downsides. Things wasnt that great tbh at home. But for me that is expected, its nothing serious as its just something to go through and learn from. Yellow bees will always search for honey while the queen sits at home. I started looking within myself deeper, it was like my world kept breaking again and again. I learned a lot in short amount of time


[08/01/2026 3:02 am] iamjustwhiteeye
Well now im 19, going onto 20 next month. 17-18 was basically the same, fucked up shit though. I was basically ridiculed for a lot of dumb stuff and pushed blame for. Ofc I had no problem with it, but it was annoying to deal with, especially with such old people. Nagging in my ear, not for my own sake, never asked me real questions or really tried to help. It was ridiculous. It wasn't a great time tbh, but through this I started to understand. I am the only one is this world who can tell and see where my path leads. I can't depend on others and cant expect anything from others, judge nothing, but observer and understand for what is. To know life is one of just the exprience, the perspective. I was a lot calmer, at that time I even look at myself back and see I was like a monk, unwavering, I didnt care for a lot of negatives, loved the positives as much as the negatives. Every fucked up situation, was just a book to read. I needed to learn and understand those times. Important lessons I needed to feel and see. At the end of 18, ofc got a job 3 months later I am what I am now. 19. Injuries, pain, insufferable pain, mental spirals due to that pain, havent had one since March or even close. Last year I think I evolved mentally, especially in understanding and just perception alone. I understood concepts, and life in ways I never could or was close to. Its as if you were touching the surface of water but never could sink your hand in. Only feeling and seeing your reflection. Deep reflection, understanding and knowing, through hardship and suffering, only going through with resilience. Something I would never ever admit to in a thousand years back. To me nothing I did was hard. I just did what I had to. No matter how much I suffered, I had to do what I had to. I had a responsibility. I had to adapt mentally and physically. And slowly i started changing because of that. I think since 18, im disconnected really, non attachment with dissociation. Not a judge, but just a observing understanding things as things went. My emotional involvement is basically to 0, and my thinking isn't clouded. But thats not without its vices. Also school for last year's of highschool was literally a chill time, met a guy, went everywhere with him, he just brung me everywhere and I followed. I had fun, I never questioned, just down for whatever. Met him in my German class. We talked and hanged out a lot, only in school. [11/01/2026 2:02 am] .looksmax
In the wild, a lion's appearance acts as a "biological billboard" that communicates its health, genetic quality, and social status. For a lioness (and for researchers), these visual cues are critical for survival and successful reproduction.

1. Physical Indicators of Health
When assessing a lion’s vitality, several "soft" and "hard" signs are used to determine its current condition:

Body Condition Score (BCS): This is a standardized scale (usually 1–5 or 1–9) used by biologists to judge fat and muscle reserves. A healthy lion has visible muscle definition in the neck, shoulders, and tail base.


Good Health: Ribs and hip bones are not prominent; the animal has a "filled out" appearance.

Poor Health: Sunken flanks, visible vertebrae, and a prominent "pin bone" at the tail base indicate malnutrition or disease.

Coat and Skin: A healthy lion has a sleek, uniform coat. Bald patches, matted fur, or heavy infestations of ticks and parasites are signs of a compromised immune system.

The "Bright Eye" Test: Clear, alert, and amber-colored eyes are indicators of health. Discharge from the eyes, nose, or ears often signals respiratory infections or other illnesses.

Dental Health: Canines are essential for hunting. Broken or rotting teeth can lead to starvation, making a "mouth check" a vital indicator of long-term survival.

2. Dimorphic Features & Mate Selection
Lions are the only cats that are truly sexually dimorphic—meaning males and females look significantly different. For a lioness, the most important dimorphic feature is the mane, which serves as a signal of "high quality."

What Females Look For:
Research by biologists like Craig Packer has shown that lionesses use the mane as a proxy for a male’s fitness.

Mane Color (The Most Important): Females have a strong preference for darker (blacker) manes.

Why? A dark mane is linked to higher testosterone levels and better nutrition. It signals that the male is a formidable fighter and is healthy enough to withstand the "heat cost" of a dark coat (dark manes absorb more heat, which can be a physical burden in the African sun).

Mane Length: While humans often associate length with beauty, lionesses actually prioritize color over length. Length is often an indicator of age and fighting success; a shorter mane might indicate a male who was recently injured, as hair growth is stunted by the stress of recovery.


Size and Presence: Males are up to 50% larger than females. A lioness looks for a male who is large enough to defend the pride and its cubs against rival "infanticidal" males.

{Forwarded Message}
@everyone IMPORTANT !

**ORG SMP Announcement**

We are creating an **ORG Minecraft SMP**.

This is a **semi-anarchy survival server** tied directly to ORG and looksmax.
**Everything allowed.**

**PvP allowed.**
**Raiding allowed.**
**Griefing allowed.**
**Scamming allowed.**
**Betrayals allowed.**

The only thing not allowed is exploits that break or crash the server.

No land claims.
No rollbacks.
No protection systems.

What happens in-game stays in-game.

**Development starts January 1st, 2026.**

---
**What this server is**

This is not a casual SMP.

This is a high-risk, player-driven server where:

* Power comes from resources, strategy, and alliances
* Losses are permanent
* Economy matters
* Reputation matters

**ORG SMP** means the economy, items, progression, and events are built around ORG and looksmax concepts.

---
**Roles**

Forum roles transfer automatically.

VIP, Lifetime VIP, Supporter sync 1:1 to the SMP.

They provide:

* Bigger daily Mogcoin payouts
* Faster mining/farming
* Reduced cooldowns
* Access to exclusive perks and shops

SMP-only roles and standalone perks will exist.

Roles do not make you immune. They only speed progression.

---
**Mogcoins**

**Mogcoins = main currency.**

Same Mogconomy used on Discord.

Your Mogcoin balance is **shared between Discord and SMP**.
One balance across both platforms.

**Win Mogcoins on Discord → they appear in-game.**
**Lose Mogcoins in-game → gone on Discord.**

---
**Earning Mogcoins**

* Mining
* Farming
* Bulk selling resources
* Trading rare items
* Killing players with bounties

Example:
**Diamond ≈ 5000 Mogcoins**

Large farms and controlled areas generate serious income.

---
**Gambling**

* Blackjack
* Roulette
* Coinflips
* Item gambling

Gamble Mogcoins or items.
High risk. No refunds.

---
**Bounties**

Players can place bounties on other players.

Pay Mogcoins to put a price on someone’s head.
Whoever kills them gets the payout.

This enables:

* Assassinations
* Group hunts
* Revenge plays
* Territory wars

---
**Custom items and peptide systems**

Peptides and compounds are a core mechanic.
They give strong temporary buffs, but **stacking or abusing them carries risk**.

**Peptides list and effects:**

* **CJC-1295**: Boosts max health and passive regen
* **HGH**: Increases mining speed, strength, and jump height
* **IGF-1**: Combat damage boost, faster attack speed
* **BPC-157**: Rapid health regen, reduced death penalties
* **TB-500**: Movement speed boost, reduced fall damage
* **MK-677**: Increases hunger efficiency and passive regen
* **GHRP-6**: Faster stamina recovery, short burst strength
* **L-Carnitine**: Movement speed and reduced exhaustion while running
* **EPO**: Longer sprint duration, faster recovery after combat
* **Sleepmax Compound**: Boosted XP gain, faster night skip effects

**Stacking & abuse consequences:**

* Overstacked peptides = stronger buffs
* **High risk of crash/debuffs**
* Addiction system: frequent use increases dependency
* Long-term effects: slower regen if abused, heart attacks, random temporary debuffs
* Death can occur from overdose

All peptides can be crafted, traded, or looted from bosses.
---
**Additional content**

* Battlepasses
* Custom mobs + bosses
* Rare drops
* Limited-time events
* Tradable and lootable items
---
**Monetization**

Some features cost Mogcoins.
Some cost real money.
---
This is an **ORG SMP**.

**Everything allowed.**
Play smart or get wiped.

[11/01/2026 7:58 pm] iamjustwhiteeye
“Studies show women aren’t satisfied with less attractive husbands”
This is a misrepresentation of the literature.

What studies actually show:
Physical attraction affects initial desire and short-term arousal

Long-term marital satisfaction correlates more strongly with:

- Emotional attunement
- Perceived fairness
- Partner responsiveness
- Trust
- Shared values
- Conflict resolution
- Large longitudinal studies (e.g., Karney & Bradbury, 1995; Gottman, 1999–2015) show:
Looks predict early attraction
They do NOT predict long term satisfaction or relationship stability.
If attractiveness determined satisfaction:
Divorce rates would be lowest among the most attractive couples (they aren’t)
Celebrity marriages would be the most stable (they aren’t)
Aging would universally destroy relationships (it doesn’t)

“Women want to fuck more attractive men”
This is a desire ≠ behavior error.

Yes:
Humans **fantasize**
Both men and women report attraction to people outside relationships

But:
**Fantasies** are not predictors of action
Sexual **desire** is not equivalent to partner preference
Partner choice ≠ momentary 
arousal

By this logic:
Men watching porn means they don’t love their partners (false)
Thoughts determine reality (they don’t)
You’re confusing sexual cognition with relational decision making. They are neurologically distinct systems. 😭

“Sex feels good because it’s for reproduction”
Again, origin ≠ meaning.

If sex were functionally reproduction:
Infertile people wouldn’t desire it (they do)
Same-sex sex wouldn’t exist (it does)
Non-fertile sex wouldn’t be common (it is)
Sexual boredom wouldn’t exist (it does)

Sex in humans functions as:
Bonding
Regulation
Validation
Identity expression
Stress relief
Intimacy
Reproduction is one function, not the definition

“Contraception isn’t natural, so it doesn’t count”
This is an appeal to nature fallacy. (Again 😂)

By this logic:
Glasses don’t count (not natural)
Medicine doesn’t count
Phones don’t count
Civilization doesn’t count
Humans **override biology constantly.**

If something has existed for Centuries, Across cultures, or At massive scale. Then it is part of human reality, regardless of evolutionary origin.
**Natural ≠ relevant.**

“Men who have sex a lot are seen with more respect than women”

This is **cultural**(something ive been pointing out), not biological. It’s called a sexual double standard, documented in sociology.

Example: In many patriarchal societies, male promiscuity is praised, female promiscuity is punished. That’s social enforcement, not a law of nature.

Evidence,
Clark & Hatfield (1989): Men and women respond differently to casual sex offers. but this is about social perception, not “biology.”
Social norms shift constantly: In some modern Western contexts, women having casual sex is normalized. In Scandinavian countries, sexual liberation is largely gender-neutral.
Biology doesn’t dictate respect. Cultural norms dictate respect. You're conflating the two. 😭😭

“It isn’t natural to not want to create babies… it’s why we exist”

This is a classic appeal to nature fallacy.

Humans don’t exist solely to reproduce. we exist as complex social beings with culture, technology, and choice. Many healthy humans choose not to have children and that doesn’t make them “unnatural” or ill. Reproduction is optional in humans. Evolution doesn’t dictate every behavior, it provides tendencies, not moral rules (and i dont even believe in morality). 😂 For example:
Some people naturally avoid sugar (survival advantage)
Some people naturally enjoy sugar (modern consequence: obesity risk)
Trying to reduce human value and morality to reproduction is over-simplistic and biologically naive.

Biology provides tendencies, not deterministic rules. Environment, culture, and individual choice dominate human outcomes.

I get it which is why BP is just extermely flawed. Using animals as proof for human social dynamics is flawed just by default.
